Furthermore, the same representations capture diachronic concept stability: variance in pairwise distances across languages yields stability rankings that correlate significantly with established rankings. Ablation studies confirm that both the language-level objective and the use of phonetic feature vectors improved the inferred trees topology with regards to GQD. The framework thus provides a computationally efficient and fully automatic alternative for large-scale phylogenetic inference and offers a unified representation supporting downstream analyses at both the language and concept level.

</details>


### [143] [LexFlip: A Dissociation Diagnostic for Legal Meaning Preservation Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05296)
*Gaurab Baral*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LexFlip数据集，用于评估法律文本简化后的语义保留能力。现有指标（如基于词重叠的指标）无法有效区分“表面相似但法律效力改变”的情况，而LexFlip通过373个最小扰动样本解决了这一解耦问题，揭示了当前嵌入和BERTScore等指标在法律力变化上的低敏感性，以及长度特征在FrJudge基准上的异常表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的检查方法（如要求相同对得分最高、无关对最低）无法验证简化法律条款是否保留了原意，因为它们将词汇重叠与法律效力捆绑在一起，导致任何基于token重叠的单调函数都能满足测试条件，从而产生虚假的正向结果。需要一种机制来解耦表面形式和法律效力。

Method: 作者发布了LexFlip，包含373个魁北克法语法规的最小扰动样本，这些样本保留了93%的token但反转了法律效力。使用此数据集作为基准，测试了七种嵌入模型和BERTScore指标，并与双向自然语言推理（NLI）及人类判断（FrJudge）进行对比。

Result: 1. LexFlip成功实现了表面形式固定而法律效力变化的解耦。2. 所测试的七种嵌入和BERTScore指标在处理此类编辑时，其得分范围仅占相同到无关范围的0.022-0.039，远低于双向NLI的0.670。3. 在FrJudge基准上，简单的长度特征超越了所有语义指标，且误差边际最低，甚至优于人类天花板水平（r=0.597）。

Conclusion: 当前的语义相似度指标（包括嵌入和BERTScore）在法律文本简化场景中表现不佳，无法有效捕捉法律效力的细微变化。相比之下，简单的长度特征在某些基准上表现出意外的高相关性，提示需要重新评估法律NLP任务的评估标准，并重视表面形式与法律意义之间的解耦。

Abstract: Does a simplified legal clause still say what the original said? The checks in current use cannot establish that it does: requiring an identical pair to score highest and an unrelated pair lowest moves lexical overlap and legal force together, so any monotone function of token overlap satisfies both. Our remedy is a dissociation, an item holding surface form fixed while legal force moves. We release LexFlip, 373 minimal perturbations of Quebec statutory French that reverse legal force while preserving 0.93 of the tokens, with a harness scoring metrics, regressors and prompted judges alike. The seven embedding and BERTScore metrics we test spend only 0.022 to 0.039 of their identical-to-unrelated range on such an edit, against 0.670 for bidirectional NLI, the one family the identical-pair check would disqualify. On FrJudge, against a measured human ceiling of r=0.597, a bare length feature outscores every semantic metric and has the lowest margin we measure.

</details>


### [144] [WearableQA: A Benchmark for Health Reasoning over Real-World Wearable Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05405)
*Ji Soo Lee,Xilun Chen,Pierce Chuang,Ashish Shenoy,Jason Wei,Dohwan Ko,Hyunwoo J. Kim,Benoit Corda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出WearableQA基准，利用200名真实用户的长期可穿戴数据构建4,084道多选题，旨在评估大语言模型（LLM）对真实世界可穿戴数据的推理能力。该基准涵盖16种问题类型，区分数据计算与生理解释、单信号与多信号整合推理。实验显示现有LLM性能差异显著（19.6%-72.9%），整体准确率低于60%，表明该任务极具挑战性且具备诊断价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管可穿戴传感技术已能连续监测生理和行为信号，但现有的基准测试很少评估AI系统是否能在真实的用户纵向可穿戴记录上进行推理。现有研究缺乏对真实世界可穿戴数据分布（包括设备噪声和个体差异）的考量，难以全面衡量模型在复杂现实场景下的推理能力。

Method: 1. **数据构建**：基于200名真实用户长达500天的日常测量数据，结合可穿戴时间序列、血液生物标志物和人口统计学信息，构建了包含4,084个10选项多选题的数据集。
2. **问题分类体系**：引入两个互补维度组织16种问题类型：一是“数据vs健康推理”（区分对纵向测量的计算与生理解释），二是“单信号vs跨信号推理”（区分单一信号分析与多信号整合）。
3. **双重 grounding 框架**：结合文献支持的生理发现和经统计验证的人口群体模式，确保问题的可靠性和现实意义。
4. **模型评估**：对14个专有和开源LLM进行测试，以10%随机猜测为基线，评估其推理性能。

Result: 1. WearableQA能有效区分不同模型的能力，性能范围在19.6%至72.9%之间。
2. 绝大多数模型的准确率低于60%，表明该基准远未被解决（far from solved）。
3. 数据集保留了真实的可穿戴数据分布特征，包括设备噪声和个体间差异性。

Conclusion: WearableQA提供了一个逼真且具有诊断意义的基准，用于评估大语言模型在真实世界可穿戴数据上的推理能力。当前大多数LLM在此任务上表现不佳，显示出巨大的提升空间，同时也证明了该基准在识别模型局限性方面的有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in wearable sensing enable continuous monitoring of physiological and behavioral signals, yet existing benchmarks rarely evaluate whether AI systems can reason over a real user's longitudinal wearable record. We introduce WearableQA, a benchmark comprising 4,084 10-option multiple-choice questions constructed from the wearable time series, blood biomarkers, and demographics of 200 real users, each with up to 500 days of daily measurements. WearableQA preserves authentic wearable distributions that include device noise and inter-individual variability. To evaluate distinct reasoning capabilities, we introduce 16 question types organized along two complementary axes: data versus health reasoning, which distinguishes computation over longitudinal measurements from physiological interpretation; and single- versus cross-signal reasoning, which separates reasoning about individual signals from the integration of multiple signals. To construct reliable questions at scale, we adopt a dual-grounding framework that combines literature-grounded physiological findings with statistically validated population-grounded physiological patterns. This enables the capture of meaningful relationships observed in real-world wearable data. Evaluation of 14 proprietary and open-source LLMs demonstrates that WearableQA effectively differentiates model capabilities, with performance ranging from 19.6% to 72.9% against a 10% chance baseline. Moreover, WearableQA remains far from solved: most models achieve accuracies below 60%. Overall, WearableQA provides a realistic and diagnostic benchmark for evaluating LLM reasoning over real-world wearable data.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Corporate-Family Resolution Is Not a String-Matching Problem: A Public Benchmark Stratified by Name Visibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04269)
*Harshit Gupta*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出CorpFam基准测试，揭示企业家族关联判定本质是检索而非匹配问题。现有方法因候选集生成（Blocking）瓶颈导致“不可见”链接召回率极低（仅6.8%），尽管这些链接真实存在且经SEC数据验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决供应商记录是否属于同一企业家族的问题，这对支出整合、信用风险聚合和制裁筛查至关重要。传统实体匹配方法失效，因为家族链接连接的是故意不同的实体，且证据往往不在记录中直接显示。

Method: 构建包含54,864个候选对、覆盖10,307个企业家族的CorpFam公开基准数据集，源自美国联邦奖项记录。采用按名称可见性分层的评估策略（完全相同、共享独特标记、无共享），报告分层召回率而非F1值。评估了5种匹配器和7种阻塞方案。

Result: 最强匹配器对完全相同的对召回率为100%，但对“不可见”对的召回率仅为4.2%。所有阻塞方案对不可见对的召回率均低于3%，其并集仅恢复6.8%。93.2%的真实链接从未进入候选集。经SEC附属公司附表验证，64.2%的“不可见”链接为真实链接，证明问题出在候选生成阶段。

Conclusion: 企业家族解析是一个被错误归类为匹配问题的检索问题，关键干预点在于候选生成（Candidate Generation）而非排序（Ranking）。研究发布了基准、审核日志及复现代码。

Abstract: Deciding whether two supplier records belong to the same corporate family is a prerequisite for spend consolidation, credit exposure aggregation and sanctions screening. It is usually treated as entity matching, but the tasks differ: a family link connects records that are deliberately different entities, and the evidence often appears in neither record. We introduce CorpFam, a public benchmark of 54,864 candidate pairs over 10,307 corporate families, derived from 6,638,350 US federal award records in which every supplier self-reports its ultimate parent to a government registry. Pairs are stratified by name visibility: whether the names are identical after normalisation, share a distinctive token, or share none. Because strata have positive rates from 10.2% to 97.3%, we report per-stratum recall, base-rate invariant, rather than F1, which is not. The strongest of 5 matchers recovers 100.0% of identical pairs and 4.2% of invisible ones; no method exceeds 4.7% on the latter. The failure begins before matching. Blocking decides which pairs a matcher sees, and we evaluate 7 schemes spanning phonetic keys, attribute keys that ignore the name, and semantic nearest neighbours. None reaches three percent on invisible pairs, and their union recovers 6.8%. 93.2% of these links never enter the candidate set, so no matching-stage improvement can reach them. The links are real: against SEC Exhibit 21 subsidiary schedules, which share no provenance with procurement registration, 64.2% of invisible links are corroborated, against 0.16% under permuted parents and 0.41% against the same parent's wrong exhibit: two unrelated nulls agreeing to within 0.25 points. Corporate-family resolution is a retrieval problem misfiled as a matching problem; the intervention point is candidate generation, not ranking. The benchmark, adjudication log, and code reproducing every number are released.

</details>


### [146] [A Semantic Model of Genetic Evidence: A Step Toward Bridging the Basic-Science-Clinic Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04509)
*Michael Bouzinier,Dmitry Etin*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出一种面向基础/临床前研究的细粒度科学证据语义模型，通过FHIR Evidence对齐和SHACL验证，在基因变异解读场景下验证了其作为AI就绪基础设施的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有证据表示标准（如FHIR Evidence, ECO, SEPIO等）主要面向临床试验工作流或单变异断言，无法捕捉基础研究和临床前研究中声明的细粒度、领域特定结构。

Method: 1. 构建包含三个核心类的科学证据语义模型；2. 针对遗传学进行专门化扩展；3. 与FHIR Evidence结构对齐，采用SEPIO锚定的可信度分解；4. 附加紧凑维度词汇，并通过SHACL模式验证条件激活规则约束；5. 在6篇遗传学论文上进行人类-AI协同标注试点研究。

Result: 生成28项证据项目和95个源锚定断言；成功区分策展人编写的参考注释与AI起草的注释；证明了模型在表示遗传证据方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该模型是构建可信赖、AI就绪的变异解释基础设施的重要增量，为表示遗传证据提供了参考数据模型和验证模式。

Abstract: Scientific and clinical decision-making depends on evidence from the primary literature, but existing standards for representing that evidence (FHIR Evidence, ECO, SEPIO, and the GA4GH Genomic Knowledge Standards) are oriented toward clinical-trial workflows, evidence codes, or single-variant assertions, and do not capture the fine-grained, domain-specific structure of claims in basic and pre-clinical research. We introduce a semantic model for scientific evidence with three core classes, specialize it for genetics, align it structurally to FHIR Evidence with a SEPIO-anchored credibility decomposition, and attach a compact dimensional vocabulary whose conditional-activation rules are validated by a SHACL schema for the implemented constraints. Using clinical variant interpretation as the driving use case, we evaluate the model through a human-AI annotation pilot over six genetics papers, yielding 28 evidence items and 95 source-anchored assertions, with a workflow that keeps curator-authored reference annotations distinct from AI-drafted annotations. Treating the pilot as a feasibility study rather than a benchmark, we argue that the model is a useful increment toward trustworthy, AI-ready infrastructure for variant interpretation: a reference data model and validation schema for representing genetic evidence.

</details>


### [147] [Reducing the Cross-Model Tax: Query Optimization over Multi-Model Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05014)
*Jáchym Bártík,Filip Štrobl,Irena Holubová*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出一种感知映射与能力的优化方法，将处理逻辑下推至数据源，显著降低跨异构数据模型查询的延迟和开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于分解的跨异构数据模型架构存在巨大开销（即“跨模型税”），研究发现这部分开销并非源于异构性本身，而是由统一查询处理器中可避免的决策导致。

Method: 提出一种统一的优化流水线，结合模型感知的谓词下推、跨模型依赖连接以及非冗余查询部分构建。该方法在 MM-quecat 系统中实现，并应用于关系型、文档型和图数据库。

Result: 在 PostgreSQL、MongoDB 和 Neo4j 及其组合上的评估显示：查询延迟最高降低两个数量级；消除了原始实验中所有内存溢出故障；通过依赖执行进一步提升性能一个数量级；复杂图计划的规划时间从数百毫秒降至数毫秒。

Conclusion: 既有的优化原则可以推广到数据模型和系统边界之外，能显著提高基于分解的多模型查询处理的效率和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Querying across heterogeneous data models incurs substantial overhead from query decomposition, data transfer, and processing outside the underlying database systems. We show that, in the evaluated decomposition-based architecture, a substantial part of this cross-model tax is not inherent to heterogeneity itself, but results from avoidable decisions made by the unifying query processor.
  We present a mapping- and capability-aware optimization approach that systematically moves processing closer to the data. It combines model-aware predicate pushdown, cross-model dependent joins, and non-redundant query-part construction within a unified optimization pipeline applicable across relational, document, and graph databases.
  The approach is implemented in MM-quecat and evaluated over PostgreSQL, MongoDB, Neo4j, and their heterogeneous combination. It reduces query latency by up to two orders of magnitude, eliminates all out-of-memory failures observed in the original single-DBMS experiments, provides further order-of-magnitude improvements through dependent execution, and reduces planning time for complex graph plans from hundreds of milliseconds to several milliseconds. The results demonstrate that established optimization principles can be generalized across data-model and system boundaries and can substantially improve the efficiency and robustness of decomposition-based multi-model query processing.

</details>


### [148] [CAT-LDP: Cloud-edge Adaptive Taxonomy under Local Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05095)
*Junzhe Yang,Chang Xia,Xiyun Wang,Anren Sun,Wenbo Ding,Xinye Chen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出CAT-LDP框架，通过云端-本地协同及自适应隐私预算分配，在局部差分隐私约束下平衡隐式反馈推荐系统的隐私保护与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私保护推荐方法难以平衡用户隐私与推荐性能，尤其在数据稀疏的隐式反馈场景下，隐私扰动导致有用信号损失严重。

Method: 构建基于局部差分隐私（LDP）的云-本地协同推荐框架。结合分层分类树与自适应隐私预算分配策略，用户上传满足LDP的扰动类别画像；云端进行粗粒度候选生成，本地设备利用未扰动的历史数据进行细粒度重排序。

Result: 在Amazon Video Games数据集上的实验表明，CAT-LDP在不同隐私预算下，于HR@K和NDCG@K指标上均优于固定预算变体及代表性基线模型。

Conclusion: 将类别空间建模与云-本地任务解耦相结合，能有效降低长尾稀疏设置下的噪声放大效应，为隐式反馈推荐提供了更好的隐私与效用平衡。

Abstract: Recommender systems are widely used in daily life, but their direct collection and use of user preference data can also lead to privacy leakage. Existing privacy-preserving recommendation methods often find it hard to balance user privacy and recommendation performance. This problem is more serious in implicit-feedback settings, where data sparsity further increases the loss of useful signals caused by privacy perturbation. To solve this problem, we propose CAT-LDP, a cloud-local collaborative recommendation framework under local differential privacy constraints. CAT-LDP combines a hierarchical taxonomy tree with an adaptive privacy budget allocation strategy to keep more useful signals in users' active categories while protecting user privacy. Specifically, users upload perturbed category profiles that satisfy LDP. Based on these profiles, the cloud performs coarse-grained candidate generation, and the local device then carries out fine-grained reranking by using unperturbed local history. Experiments on the Amazon Video Games dataset show that CAT-LDP consistently outperforms its fixed-budget ablation variant and representative baselines on HR@K and NDCG@K under different privacy budgets. The results show that combining category-space modeling with cloud-local task decoupling can effectively reduce noise amplification in long-tail sparse settings and provide a better balance between privacy and utility for implicit-feedback recommendation.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [SAGE: Semantic Attribute Graphs for Multi-Entity Visual Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04255)
*Yongjoo Kim,Mincheol Kwon,Seonga Choi,Minseung Lee,Kyeong-Jin Oh,Hyunyoung Lee,Yunsu Choi,Jungbeom Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对密集文档图像检索中的“语义稀释”问题，提出无训练框架SAGE，通过解析语义实体并构建分层图进行子图匹配，在DEAR数据集上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准视觉-语言检索器将裁剪区域编码为单一向量，导致密集文档中不同实体的信号混合，产生“语义稀释”，从而模糊了细粒度检索所需的证据，降低检索性能。

Method: 提出SAGE框架：1) 从密集文档图像中解析语义实体；2) 使用多向量嵌入将实体表示为分层图节点；3) 通过迭代实体级子图匹配检索与查询相关的证据。同时发布DEAR数据集（1,055个查询-图像对）。

Result: SAGE显著减少了语义稀释，在DEAR数据集上优于基于补丁和OCR的检索基线。在多实体视觉比较查询中，Recall@3达到0.849，生成得分为2.746。

Conclusion: SAGE通过结构化实体表示和子图匹配有效解决了密集文档检索中的语义稀释问题，提升了细粒度实体检索和比较的能力。

Abstract: Dense document images often contain many fine-grained visual and textual entities whose relevance depends on a user query. Standard vision-language retrievers encode cropped regions with a single vector, which can mix distinct entity signals and obscure the evidence needed for fine-grained retrieval. We call this failure mode Semantic Dilution and quantitatively show that it degrades entity-level retrieval as a function of entity density. To mitigate it, we propose SAGE, a training-free framework that parses semantic entities from dense document images, represents them as hierarchical graph nodes with multi-vector embeddings, and retrieves query-relevant evidence through iterative entity-level subgraph matching. We also introduce DEAR, a dataset of 1,055 query--image pairs sourced from product detail pages, where each query requires retrieving and comparing multiple fine-grained entities from visually dense inputs across four question types of increasing complexity. Experiments show that SAGE substantially reduces semantic dilution and outperforms patch-level and OCR-based retrieval baselines on DEAR, achieving a Recall@3 of 0.849 and a generation score of 2.746 on multi-entity visual comparison queries. Our code is available at https://github.com/All4Nothing/SAGE.

</details>


### [150] [MURAL: Multimodal Uncertainty-aware Recommendation via Adaptive edge Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04574)
*Ahmad Mousavi,Majid Alikhani,Yeon-Chang Lee,Roberto Corizzo,Yeganeh Abdollahinejad*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出MURAL框架，通过自适应边学习和不确定性感知融合，解决多模态推荐中结构僵化和语义脆弱性问题，在大规模数据集上显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态图神经网络推荐系统面临两大瓶颈：1. 结构僵化（依赖静态预计算相似度图，无法适应动态偏好）；2. 语义脆弱性（噪声模态信号被无差别融合，扭曲协同信号）。

Method: 1. 自适应边学习器：结合可微检索增强策略与近似最近邻搜索，发现潜在且语义自适应的物品-物品相关性（复杂度O(NlogN)）；2. 不确定性感知融合模块：建模异构模态的偶然不确定性，动态降低不可靠特征权重，优先保留高置信度信号以抵御跨模态噪声；3. 对比教师-学生对齐：将模态特定表示锚定到稳定的行为信号，确保优化稳定性且无梯度泄漏。

Result: 在TikTok和Amazon等大规模基准测试中，MURAL显著优于结构和生成类最先进基线模型，实现了更高的准确率，并通过特定领域模态主导性提供可解释性，且在极端数据损坏下表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MURAL通过将多模态推荐从固定的结构增强转向动态拓扑发现，有效解决了结构僵化和语义脆弱性问题，提升了推荐的准确性、可解释性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multimodal Graph Neural Networks have become standard for recommendation by augmenting sparse interaction data with content features. Yet current architectures face two bottlenecks: structural rigidity, from a reliance on static precomputed similarity graphs that cannot adapt to evolving preferences; and semantic fragility, where noisy modality signals are indiscriminately fused, distorting the collaborative signal. We propose MURAL (Multimodal Uncertainty-aware Recommendation via Adaptive edge Learning), a unified framework that shifts multimodal recommendation from fixed structural augmentation to dynamic topology discovery. To address structural rigidity, an Adaptive Edge Learner combines a differentiable retrieval-augmented strategy with an approximate nearest neighbor search to discover latent item-item correlations that are both semantically adaptive and computationally scalable (O(NlogN)). To address semantic fragility, an Uncertainty-Aware Fusion module models the aleatoric uncertainty of heterogeneous modalities, dynamically down-weighting unreliable features while prioritizing high-confidence signals as a defense against cross-modal noise. We further employ a contrastive teacher-student alignment that anchors modality-specific representations to stable behavioral signals, ensuring optimization stability without gradient leakage. Experiments on large-scale benchmarks including TikTok and Amazon show that MURAL significantly surpasses both structural and generative state-of-the-art baselines, achieving superior accuracy while offering interpretability through domain-specific modality dominance and robustness under extreme data corruption.

</details>


### [151] [Latent-Aligned Reasoning for Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04645)
*Jiarui Jin,Anyang Ji*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出LARK框架，通过两阶段潜在推理和互补对齐机制解决推荐场景中多模态信号衰减问题，实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在推荐应用中面临“跨模态稀释”挑战：随着多步推理过程中表示的传播，视觉和文本信号逐渐减弱。

Method: 提出LARK（Latent-Aligned Reasoning frameworK），一种包含互补对齐机制的两阶段潜在推理框架：第一阶段将可学习的潜在标记与链式思维（CoT）推理交错，并与冻结的视觉编码器显式对齐以保留感知细节；第二阶段通过桥接MLP投影潜在表示，使用物品到物品的对比学习训练，并将中间特征与第一阶段的CoT隐藏状态对齐以防止语义 fading。

Result: 在三个公开基准和一个工业数据集上，LARK在多种推荐架构中实现了最先进的（SOTA）性能，消融实验证实了各组件的独特贡献。

Conclusion: LARK通过有效的潜在对齐机制成功缓解了跨模态稀释问题，提升了多模态推荐系统的性能。

Abstract: Multimodal Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in cross-modal understanding, yet a fundamental challenge persists when applying them to recommendation: as representations propagate through multi-step reasoning, both visual and textual signals progressively attenuate - a phenomenon we term cross-modal dilution. To address this, we propose LARK (Latent-Aligned Reasoning frameworK), a two-stage latent reasoning framework with complementary alignment mechanisms within a single VLM. In the first stage, learnable latent tokens are interleaved with multi-step chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning and explicitly aligned with a frozen vision encoder, serving as visual checkpoints that preserve perceptual details throughout the reasoning chain. In the second stage, the latent representations are projected via a bridge MLP and trained with item-to-item contrastive learning; to prevent the reasoning semantics from fading, intermediate features are aligned with the CoT hidden states from the first stage, anchoring the final embeddings to the model's own reasoning output. Experiments on three public benchmarks and one industrial dataset show that LARK achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple recommendation architectures, with controlled ablations confirming the distinct contribution of each component.

</details>


### [152] [VizIt: A multi-view framework for exploring single-cell, spatial, and genetic data online](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04658)
*Chenhang Christopher Zhang,Yanqing Lou,Jie Yuan,Mingming Lu,Jacob Parker,Himanshu Chintalapudi,Zechuan Lin,Clemens R. Scherzer,Yuxuan Hu,Ruifeng Hu,Xianjun Dong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出VizIt框架，实现单细胞、空间转录组等多模态数据的交互式多视图探索，解决数据探索碎片化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多组学研究中需从互补生物学视角审视数据，但现有工具导致跨模态和工具的交互探索分散且割裂。

Method: 开发开源框架VizIt，连接基因、细胞类型、条件、空间、基因组区域及变异中心等不同视角的视图，支持在生物学视角间无缝导航。

Result: 通过帕金森氏症细胞图谱（Parkinson's Cell Atlas）展示了VizIt作为可定制交互式多组学资源的应用能力。

Conclusion: VizIt为整合和探索多模态生物数据提供了有效的交互式解决方案，促进了多组学研究中的综合分析。

Abstract: Multi-omic studies increasingly require data to be examined from complementary biological perspectives, yet interactive exploration remains fragmented across modalities and tools. We present VizIt, an open-source framework for multi-view exploration of single-cell and spatial transcriptomic, epigenomic and genetic data. VizIt connects gene-, cell type-, condition-, spatial-, genomic region- and variant-centered views, enabling seamless navigation across biological perspectives. We demonstrate VizIt through the Parkinson's Cell Atlas, a customizable interactive multi-omic resource.

</details>


### [153] [Inventory-Grounded Policy-Level Optimization for Training-Free AI Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04813)
*Wei Zhou,Tiandeng Wu,Jiandong Ding,Zhufeng Fan,Yi Cao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出IGPO，一种无需训练的AI搜索策略优化方法，通过分离策略与环境事实，动态结合运行时库存信息提升搜索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期部署中AI搜索面临商品目录频繁更新的问题，传统微调、强化学习和静态提示因标签稀缺、奖励漂移、发布成本高及提示失效快而难以适用。

Method: IGPO将策略与环境事实解耦：离线时通过随机 rollout 生成对比信号并区分检索失败与无匹配情况；在线时探测库存构建画像，注入相关策略指南到检索和选择提示中。

Result: 自2026年5月商用部署以来，14天A/B测试显示相对点击率（CTR）提升3.17%，审核不良案例减少38.9%。

Conclusion: IGPO有效解决了动态库存下AI搜索策略的稳定性问题，显著提升了商业搜索系统的性能指标。

Abstract: Early in deployment, an AI search system typically operates over a frequently updated product catalog, so the available items and their properties cannot be treated as stable knowledge that can be encoded in fixed prompts or strategies. Fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and static prompt patches fit poorly: labels are scarce, rewards drift with inventory, model releases are costly, and prompt fixes quickly stale. We present Inventory-Grounded Policy-Level Optimization (IGPO), a training-free approach for fixed AI search pipelines. IGPO separates policy from environment facts: it learns Policy Guidelines for acting on runtime inventory evidence rather than memorizing available items. Online, IGPO grounds each query by probing the inventory and constructing an inventory portrait, then injects relevant Policy Guidelines into the retrieval and selection prompts. Offline, stochastic rollouts are grouped by query -- mixed outcome groups directly yield contrastive signal, and an inventory-guided exploration loop distinguishes missed retrieval routes from cases where no matching support is found under the observed inventory evidence. Since May 2026, IGPO has been deployed in a commercial smart-assistant AI search system. A 14-day online A/B test of the complete IGPO treatment shows a 3.17% relative CTR lift and a 38.9% reduction in audited bad cases.

</details>


### [154] [Personalized Task Dependency Graphs for Mitigating Signal Erosion in Multi-Task Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04862)
*Fuyuan Liu,Tiandeng Wu,Yaqun Fang,Wei Zhou,Zehao Zhou,Wenping Chen,Qishun Mei,Jiaxin Zhou,Heng Chang,Yi Cao,Jiandong Ding*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出PTDG模型，通过动态重构任务依赖关系和自适应掩码策略，解决工业推荐中多目标学习里的信号衰减问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多任务学习（MTL）采用静态漏斗结构，忽略了任务间相关性随物品特性变化的事实；固定的层级消息传递导致深层稀疏目标的信号严重衰减。

Method: 1. 构建个性化任务依赖图（PTDG），在保留物理因果约束前提下，利用低秩近似动态调整各物品的依赖路径强度；2. 基于GCN实现带硬因果掩码的自适应信息传播；3. 引入自适应渐进掩码（APM）策略，根据任务稀疏度解耦共享参数以稳定优化。

Result: 在KuaiRand1K和工业数据集上，PTDG使稀疏转换任务的AUC提升最高达1.45%，同时在密集目标上保持相当性能；线上A/B测试显示转化率（CVR）提升1.2%，eCPM提升1.9%。

Conclusion: PTDG通过动态适应物品特性的任务依赖结构和优化策略，有效缓解了信号侵蚀，显著提升了工业推荐系统的多目标转化效果。

Abstract: Optimizing multiple conversion objectives is a core challenge in industrial recommendation, often limited by signal erosion in rigid architectures. Existing Multi-Task Learning (MTL) methods typically enforce uniform dependency strengths across a static conversion funnel, overlooking how task correlations naturally vary based on item characteristics. Hierarchical message passing along these fixed chains leads to cumulative signal attenuation, which degrades performance on sparse, deep-funnel objectives. To address this, we propose the Personalized Task Dependency Graphs (PTDG). While respecting necessary physical causal constraints (e.g., Click -> Pay), PTDG dynamically "rewires" the intensity of dependency pathways for each item via low-rank approximation to ensure structural robustness. We implement a GCN-based propagation with hard causal masking to establish adaptive information shortcuts. Additionally, we introduce an Adaptive Progressive Masking (APM) strategy that decouples shared parameters according to task sparsity, helping to stabilize optimization. Experiments on KuaiRand1K and an industrial dataset show that PTDG significantly improves AUC on sparse conversion tasks by up to 1.45%, while maintaining comparable performance on dense objectives. Online A/B testing shows PTDG improves Conversion Rate (CVR) by 1.2% and effective Cost Per Mille (eCPM) by 1.9% relative to the baseline.

</details>


### [155] [AtomRec: Evolving Atomic Memory for Agentic Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04882)
*Peiyu Hu,Weihai Lu,Siying Gu,Zhuodong Liu,Zhaokai Luo,Yuean Niu,Zhiyong Wang,Jia Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出AtomRec，一种基于演化原子协作记忆的代理推荐系统，通过结构化原子单元和多跳证据路径解决现有机制难以保留细粒度偏好和可解释证据的问题，在四个基准上平均相对提升8.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆机制将用户和项目信息压缩为粗略摘要，并通过标量协作链接连接，导致难以保留细粒度的偏好阶段或在用户兴趣演变时检索可解释的证据。

Method: 提出AtomRec，将用户和项目记忆表示为结构化的原子单元，构建相关记忆间的语义链接，并在有新交互到达时演化相关的历史领域；在推荐过程中，检索链接的记忆作为多跳证据路径而非孤立的邻居摘要，以支持基于证据的排序。

Result: 在四个公共基准上的实验表明，AtomRec一致优于最先进的代理和增强记忆的基线方法，各项指标的平均相对提升约为8.5%。

Conclusion: AtomRec通过演化原子协作记忆和多跳证据检索，有效提升了推荐系统的性能、细粒度偏好保留能力及证据的可解释性。

Abstract: Agentic recommender systems use large language models to maintain semantic memory and support evidence-aware recommendation. However, existing memory mechanisms often compress user and item information into coarse summaries and connect them with scalar collaborative links, making it difficult to preserve fine-grained preference stages or retrieve interpretable evidence as user interests evolve. We propose \textsc{AtomRec}, an agentic recommender with evolving atomic collaborative memory. \textsc{AtomRec} represents user and item memories as structured atomic units, builds semantic links across related memories, and evolves related historical fields when new interactions arrive. During recommendation, it retrieves linked memories as multi-hop evidence paths rather than isolated neighbor summaries, allowing collaborative signals to support grounded ranking. Experiments on four public benchmarks show that \textsc{AtomRec} consistently outperforms state-of-the-art agentic and memory-augmented baselines, with around 8.5\% average relative improvement across metrics.

</details>


### [156] [SAM-D2Q: Aligning Multimodal Doc2Query with Search Demand and Conversion for E-commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04961)
*Hui Zhou,Jian Hui Ji,Lei Ma,Rong Xiao,Xiaoyi Zeng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出多模态文档扩展框架SAM-D2Q，通过结合视觉语言理解和强化学习偏好对齐，解决电商搜索中的词汇不匹配问题，显著提升检索性能和商业指标（GMV +3.38%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Doc2Query方法仅基于文本，无法充分利用产品图像的关键属性，且未针对电商商业目标优化，导致生成的伪查询虽语义合理但商业效果不佳。

Method: 构建三阶段SAM-D2Q框架：(1) 任务适应的多模态监督微调以增强图文理解；(2) 多模态数据增强以提升视觉属性感知和覆盖度；(3) 基于强化学习的偏好对齐以匹配用户意图和商业价值。

Result: 离线实验显示检索性能显著优于传统Doc2Query；线上部署后，GMV提升3.38%，支付次数提升2.27%。

Conclusion: SAM-D2Q通过多模态融合与业务目标对齐，有效解决了电商搜索中的词汇鸿沟，实现了检索效果与商业价值的双重提升。

Abstract: E-commerce search often suffers from vocabulary mismatch between user queries and merchant-authored product titles, since short titles cannot fully cover diverse user expressions or visual product attributes. Although Doc2Query alleviates this issue by generating pseudo-queries for document expansion, traditional methods are text-only and not optimized for e-commerce business objectives. As a result, they may produce semantically plausible but commercially ineffective expansions and miss key attributes present in product images. To this end, we propose E-commerce Search-Aligned Multimodal Doc2Query (SAM-D2Q), a business-aligned multimodal document expansion framework for e-commerce search under Boolean retrieval constraints. SAM-D2Q consists of three stages: (1) task-adapted multimodal supervised fine-tuning to enhance vision-language understanding of product titles, images, and user queries; (2) multimodal data augmentation to improve perception of key visual attributes and expansion coverage; and (3) reinforcement-learning-based preference alignment toward search business objectives, encouraging the model to generate pseudo-queries that better match user intent and commercial value. Offline experiments show that SAM-D2Q substantially improves retrieval performance over traditional Doc2Query methods. Deployed in the AliExpress production search system, SAM-D2Q improves online business metrics, increasing GMV by +3.38% and Pay Count by +2.27%.

</details>


### [157] [Repeated Queries Exhaust an LLM's Brand Recommendations but Not Its Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05059)
*Dmitrij Żatuchin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究对比了有无检索功能的大语言模型在重复相同购买问题时的品牌推荐行为。发现无检索模型持续生成新品牌，未出现饱和；而有检索功能的模型较快停止输出新品牌。结论是开放提取方法比固定名单追踪更能真实反映模型的多样性积累过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究重复相同的购买问题是否会耗尽大语言模型的品牌推荐能力，以及检索机制（如联网搜索）如何影响这一过程的饱和度和多样性。

Method: 对300个问题-引擎单元进行测试（50个问题，6个引擎，每个运行15次），涉及1,470个经过裁决的组织。使用开放提取方法分析品牌推荐，并采用精确稀有化曲线和Chao2丰富度估计量进行统计评估，同时通过平行固定名单提取进行对比验证。

Result: 1. 无检索的五个引擎在第15次运行时仍在86-92%的情况下添加从未见过的新品牌，中位品牌库为15-31个组织。
2. 唯一的检索增强引擎在中位数8个组织时趋于饱和，但仍有64%的情况继续添加，且早期深度测试显示其由第10次运行起饱和。
3. 引用域的积累在所有测试 horizon 上持续上升，即使在第24次运行仍有增加，且Chao2下界估计值仅观察到59-84%。
4. 单次运行即可展现五轮运行的62-77%品牌集，中位问题抽取38个组织，其中15个仅出现在一个引擎中。
5. 固定名单提取导致平坦曲线（虚假平台期），而开放提取揭示了持续的多样性增长。

Conclusion: 大语言模型的品牌推荐能力是否“耗尽”高度依赖于是否使用检索技术。无检索模型表现出持续的多样性扩展，而未达到真正的饱和状态；检索模型则更快收敛。此外，评估方法的选择（开放提取 vs 固定名单）显著影响对模型能力边界的判断，开放提取能更准确地揭示模型潜在的知识广度。

Abstract: Whether repeated identical buying questions exhaust a language model's brand recommendations depends on retrieval. Across 300 question-engine cells (50 questions, six engines, 15 runs each, open extraction over 1,470 adjudicated organizations), the five engines answering without web search were still adding never-seen brands at run 15 in 86-92% of cells, with median repertoires of 15-31 organizations; the one retrieval-enabled engine closed its list (median 8 organizations, 64% of cells still adding), matching four earlier deep cells where web-search runs saturated by run ten. Cited-domain accumulation keeps rising at every horizon tested: four deep cells were still adding domains at run 24 with 59-84% of the Chao2 lower-bound estimate observed, and 44% of the retrieval engine's breadth cells were still adding domains at run 15. A single run shows 62-77% of the five-run brand set, and across engines the median question draws 38 organizations, of which a median of 15 appear in exactly one engine. Estimators are exact rarefaction and Chao2 richness; a parallel fixed-roster extraction reproduces flat curves on identical responses, so roster-bounded tracking manufactures plateaus that open extraction removes.

</details>


### [158] [Beyond Co-purchase Relation: Evolution of Complementary Recommendations at Allegro](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05063)
*Aleksandra Osowska-Kurczab,Klaudia Nazarko,Eliška Kosturová,Lidia Wojciechowska,Michał Bień*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Allegro.com部署的生产级互补产品推荐框架AlleCompanion，通过结合数据过滤、类别约束的双塔架构及多源映射ComCat，有效区分“共同购买”与“真正互补”，显著提升GMV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准模型无法区分“仅共同购买”与“真正协同工作”商品的问题，克服大规模共购行为信号中的内在噪声，实现精准的商品篮构建。

Method: 提出AlleCompanion框架：1) 结合数据层启发式过滤与类别约束的双塔架构；2) 使用Category Adapter在嵌入空间限制候选项逻辑边界；3) 引入ComCat多源互补类别映射，整合专家规则、人机反馈、LLM推理和统计挖掘以提炼模式。

Result: 实验证明显式类别约束与神经网络结合能有效过滤共购噪声。该框架服务每月超2000万活跃用户，显著提升了自然发现的归因GMV及赞助展示的营收增长。

Conclusion: 将显式的类别级约束与神经架构相结合，是解决大规模行为噪声并满足真实用户需求的有效互补推荐策略。

Abstract: When a customer adds a professional camera to their cart, should the system suggest a matching lens, a generic tripod, or another camera body? Complementary Product Recommendation is vital for comprehensive basket building, yet standard models often fail to distinguish between items that are merely bought together and those that truly work together. In this paper, we present AlleCompanion: a production-scale retrieval framework deployed at Allegro.com that transforms noisy behavioural signals into precise semantic compatibility. We mitigate the intrinsic noise in large-scale co-purchase traffic by combining data-level filtering heuristics with a category-constrained Two Tower architecture. Within this framework, the Category Adapter guides the model in the embedding space, constraining candidates within logically complementary boundaries. Since modelling authentic user behaviour at scale is inherently difficult, we introduce ComCat, a multi-source Complementary Categories Mapping. ComCat acts as a translational layer that distils meaningful patterns from noisy traffic into a maintainable and controllable solution, integrating expert rules, human-in-the-loop feedback, LLM-based reasoning, and statistical mining. Our experimental results demonstrate that combining explicit category-level constraints with neural architectures effectively filters out co-purchase noise to surface recommendations that satisfy real-world user needs. Serving over 20 million active users monthly, the framework delivers significant uplifts in attributed GMV for organic discovery and drives substantial revenue growth in sponsored placements.

</details>


### [159] [Embedding Surgery: Localized Updates for Adaptive Ranking Correction in Dense Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05110)
*Maddalena Amendola,Antonio Mallia,Raffaele Perego*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出“嵌入手术”（Embedding Surgery），一种轻量级方法，通过在查询时对选定的文档嵌入进行局部、最小化更新，以利用反馈实现密集检索中的自适应排名校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的密集检索系统因文档嵌入离线计算且存储于静态索引中，难以适应用户反馈或不断演变的搜索意图。

Method: 将嵌入手术建模为凸优化问题，在满足排名约束的同时最小化对受影响文档表示的修改；该方法由编辑反馈、用户交互或大语言模型生成的伪标签指导，并集成到标准密集检索管道中。

Result: 在TREC Deep Learning, TREC Robust, TREC CAsT和MS MARCO基准测试上取得一致改进（例如DL-Hard下nDCG@10相对提升高达60.64%）；具有低计算成本，不破坏嵌入空间全局结构，且可通过简单原地覆盖安全高效地应用于可扩展的近邻索引。

Conclusion: 嵌入手术能有效应对噪声或变化的反馈，与查询适应方法（如CoRocchio）互补并带来额外收益，是一种高效、鲁棒的密集检索排名校正方案。

Abstract: Dense retrieval systems are core components of modern search engines, recommendation platforms, and retrieval-augmented generation pipelines. They encode documents and queries into dense embeddings, enabling efficient semantic search via vector similarity. However, because document embeddings are computed offline and stored in static indexes, these systems struggle to adapt to user feedback or evolving search intent. To address this limitation, we introduce \emph{embedding surgery}, a lightweight approach for adaptive ranking correction in dense retrieval. The method applies localized, minimal updates to selected document embeddings at query time, guided by editorial feedback, user interactions, or pseudo-labels from large language models. We formulate embedding surgery as a convex optimization problem that enforces ranking constraints while minimizing modifications to the affected document representations. We integrate embedding surgery into standard dense retrieval pipelines and evaluate it on TREC Deep Learning, TREC Robust, TREC CAsT, and MS MARCO benchmarks. Results show consistent improvements (e.g., up to +60.64\% relative improvement in nDCG@10 on DL-Hard under editorial feedback), even under noisy or shifting feedback, with low computational cost and without disrupting the global structure of the embedding space. Extensive experiments show that ranking corrections propagate to semantically related queries and that embedding updates can be applied safely and efficiently to scalable Approximate Nearest Neighbor indexes via simple in-place overwriting, without requiring costly index reconstruction. Finally, embedding surgery complements query adaptation methods such as CoRocchio, yielding additional gains while being more robust to noisy feedback.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [EXAONE Forecast for Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04239)
*Seunghan Lee,Jaehoon Lee,Jun Seo,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Minjae Kim,Sungdong Yoo,Junhyeok Kang,Sangjun Han,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EXAONE Finance，一种专为金融时间序列预测设计的无注意力基础模型，通过线性时间算子和掩码上下文增强解决长序列、多变量及缺失数据问题，在FinVerse基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用时间序列基础模型（TSFM）依赖计算成本高的自注意力机制，且假设输入完整，无法有效处理金融市场中常见的长序列、多通道、间歇性观测及大量缺失数据等独特动态。

Method: 1. 采用无注意力架构，使用因果一维卷积进行时间混合，以及组感知池化多层感知机（MLP）进行变量混合，实现线性时间复杂度；2. 引入掩码上下文增强技术，模拟连续缺失片段以提升对金融数据缺失的鲁棒性；3. 在涵盖股票、外汇、商品、加密货币、固定收益及宏观经济指标的大规模金融语料库上进行预训练。

Result: 在涵盖多种资产类别的FinVerse金融预测基准测试中，EXAONE Finance取得了最先进的性能，在点预测准确性、横截面资产排名和组合盈利能力三个评估层级均排名第一。

Conclusion: EXAONE Finance通过改进的架构设计和针对金融特性的预训练策略，成功克服了通用TSFM在金融领域的应用局限，证明了其在复杂金融预测任务中的优越性和广泛适用性。

Abstract: This technical report presents EXAONE Forecast for Finance (EXAONE Finance), a financial time series (TS) foundation model (TSFM) tailored to financial forecasting. Recent TSFMs achieve strong zero-shot performance through large-scale pretraining. However, they are primarily developed for general-domain TS and largely rely on self-attention backbones whose computational cost grows quadratically with sequence length and variate count. Moreover, they assume fully observed inputs and are pretrained on corpora that fail to capture the unique dynamics of financial markets. These limitations hinder their applicability to finance, where long, many-channel, intermittently observed panels are common. To address these challenges, EXAONE Finance adopts an attention-free architecture, replacing self-attention with two simple yet effective linear-time operators: 1) a causal 1D convolution for temporal mixing and 2) a group-aware pooling multi-layer perceptron (MLP) for variate mixing. Furthermore, a masked context augmentation exposes the model to contiguous missing spans during training, improving robustness to the missingness pervasive in financial markets. EXAONE Finance is pretrained on a large-scale financial corpus covering not only equities but also foreign exchange, commodities, crypto-assets, fixed income, and macroeconomic indicators. On FinVerse, a financial forecasting benchmark covering diverse asset classes, EXAONE Finance attains state-of-the-art performance, ranking first across all three evaluation tiers---point-forecast accuracy, cross-sectional asset ranking, and portfolio profitability.

</details>


### [161] [From Matching Models to Recruiting Agents: A Systematized Narrative Review of AI Recruitment Systems, Evaluation, and Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04286)
*Ziyi Zhao,Guanzheng Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过系统综述分析了AI招聘从简单匹配向多阶段智能体工作流的演变，指出了当前研究在证据有效性、隐私评估及公平性综合衡量上的不足，并提出了基于证据的分层评估框架和面向可审计、可争议决策的未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能在招聘中的应用已从简单的简历配对转向复杂的多阶段工作流（包括检索证据、比较候选人等）。为了理清这一发展脉络，识别现有研究的局限，并为未来建立更可靠、透明的评估体系提供依据，本文旨在对AI招聘系统进行系统性的叙事综述。

Method: 采用目的性搜索和编码协议，截至2026年9月2日，筛选并组织了40篇代表性文献，辅以工业界和法律来源。重点分析三个耦合转型：从相似度到互惠适用性、从单一模型到复合工作流、从离线预测到基于证据的生产力对齐评估。同时区分不同层级（字段、成对、列表、案例、轨迹、结果）的证据类型。

Result: 1. 识别出三大转型路径；2. 发现持久性差距：行为标签混淆了曝光度、偏好和资格；私有/合成数据限制外部效度；最终输出分数掩盖了流水线失败；3. 在编码样本中，隐私未被直接评估，且没有研究同时综合评估效用、公平性、隐私和安全性。

Conclusion: 提出从评估证据到最强可辩护主张的分层映射方法，倡导建立互惠的、基于证据的、时间可控的、选择性和可审计的系统。未来的进步应通过工作流是否能检索正确证据、保留不确定性、支持可争议决策以及在明确成本和风险约束下改善结果来评判。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence in recruitment has shifted the object being automated from profile pairs and ranked lists to multi-stage workflows that retrieve evidence, compare candidates, and support or execute actions. This systematized narrative review traces that development from bilateral retrieval and behavioral ranking through neural person--job matching, large language model (LLM) components, and tool-using recruiting agents. Using a purposive search and coding protocol updated through 23 July 2026, plus targeted updates through 2 September 2026, we organize 40 representative works with supporting industrial and legal sources. This synthesis is not a prevalence estimate. We analyze three coupled transitions: from similarity to reciprocal suitability, from a model to a compound workflow, and from offline prediction to evidence- and productivity-aligned evaluation. Across document understanding, retrieval, ranking, assessment, interviewing, sourcing, and human handoff, we distinguish field-, pair-, list-, case-, trajectory-, and outcome-level evidence. Persistent gaps arise because behavioral labels confound exposure, preference, and qualification; private and synthetic data limit external validity; final-output scores conceal pipeline failures; and, within the coded set, privacy is not directly evaluated and no row jointly evaluates utility, fairness, privacy, and security. These observations describe the coded set rather than the field as a whole. We therefore introduce a staged mapping from evaluation evidence to the strongest defensible claim, together with an agenda for reciprocal, evidence-grounded, temporally controlled, selective, and auditable systems. Progress should be judged by whether workflows retrieve the right evidence, preserve uncertainty, support contestable decisions, and improve outcomes under explicit cost and risk constraints.

</details>


### [162] [Harbor Adapters and Harbor-Index: Infrastructure and a Curated Meta-Dataset for Large-Scale Agentic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04298)
*Lin Shi,Haowei Lin,Zixuan Zhu,Xiaoyue Zhou,Xiang Li,Xiangning Lin,Yaxuan Deng,Han Xu,Yuangang Li,Shanda Li,Zizhao Chen,Hanwen Xing,Harsh Raj,Bo Chen,Quan Shi,Steven Dillmann,Yipeng Gao,Puneesh Khanna,Ruofan Lu,Chao Beyond Zhou,Michael Yang,Robert Zhang,Siyuan Chai,Jiayu Chang,Yizhao Chen,Xiaokun Chen,Yiwei Dai,Wenting Yang,Hange Liu,Minghao Liu,Zihan Wang,Adnan El Assadi,Benedikt Stroebl,E. Kelly Buchanan,Han Meng,Junwei He,Longxuan Yu,Radin Shayanfar,Yukyung Lee,Zhikang Dong,Allen G Hart,Anjiang Wei,Anurag Kashyap,Arpandeep Khatua,Audrey Jixin Zheng,Chengrui Ma,David Heineman,Dubing Chen,Hai-Anh Trinh,Haishuo Fang,Hefan Zhang,Hui Shen,Issa Sugiura,Jiankai Sun,Jiechao Gao,Junhong Lin,Junnan Li,Kai Yang,Lei Hsiung,Maoyu Wang,Mengze Tang,Nabil Omi,Negin Raoof,Nicholas Edwards,Octavia Guo,Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis,Pengliang Ji,Przemysław Hejman,Qi Qi,Qunshu Lin,Richard Zhuang,Rui Yang,Ruichen Zheng,Ryan Marten,Shaghayegh Fazliani,Shizheng Hou,Sicong Jiang,Sijie Li,Song Bian,Terry Yue Zhuo,Tianqing Wu,Tom Tang,Wanjia Zhao,Weihao Xuan,Wenhua Liang,Xian Liu,Xin Lan,Xuan Zhang,Xuandong Zhao,Yanchuan Tang,Yifan Jiang,Yijiang Li,Yitong Guan,Yizhi Li,Yonghui Liu,Yuheng Tang,Yujun,Mao,Yunfei Zhao,Yuxin Wang,Yuxuan Tang,Zhenheng Tang,Zhifei Li,Ziruo Wang,Ziyu She,Kaiyuan Liu,Iheb Chaabane,Yuxin Tang,Xiangyi Li,Andy Konwinski,Boxuan Li,Leon Liangyu Chen,Alex Dimakis,Nicholas Carlini,Soroush Vosoughi,Di He,Etash Guha,Benjamin Feuer,Mike Merrill,Ludwig Schmidt,Alex Shaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Harbor Adapters 统一评估基础设施，将80+基准适配至任意Agent，发布涵盖54个基准的大规模评估结果及精选的 Harbor-Index（82个高难度任务），以解决Agent评估复杂且缺乏统一标准的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Agent基准测试面临环境复杂、集成困难等挑战，导致评估任意Agent变得极具挑战性，缺乏统一且可扩展的评估框架。

Method: 1. 开发基准适配器（Benchmark Adapters），通过代码审查和对齐实验验证，将80多个基准适配到统一接口；2. 对8个不同能力层级的模型在54个基准上进行大规模评估，对比 Terminus-2 与3种原生Harness的效果；3. 构建 Harbor-Index，通过难度过滤、AI/人工审计及“审计-修复”循环，从适配套件中精选出82个高质量、高难度任务。

Result: 实现了跨模型的广泛能力分析与失败模式研究；Harbor-Index 保持了高挑战性与广度，同时降低运行成本，所有模型配置通过率不超过30%，最强配置（GPT-5.5 with Codex）仅达28.0%；开源了适配器、评估结果、深度分析及 Harbor-Index。

Conclusion: Harbor Adapters 及其衍生的 Harbor-Index 为语言模型Agent提供了更可靠、全面的评估支持，推动了该领域标准化评估的发展。

Abstract: Evaluating agents on the growing number of agentic benchmarks is challenging because they often require complex environments and agent integrations. We introduce Harbor Adapters, a unified evaluation infrastructure for agentic benchmarks. Our work makes three contributions. First, we develop benchmark adapters that port more than 80 benchmarks to evaluate arbitrary agents, and validate them through rigorous code review and parity experiments. Second, we conduct a large-scale evaluation of 8 models spanning capability tiers across 54 benchmarks; every model is run with Terminus-2 and with one of 3 native harnesses. This enables a broader analysis of agent capabilities and failure modes than was previously possible. Third, we introduce Harbor-Index, a curated set of 82 difficult, diverse, and high-quality tasks spanning 29 benchmarks, refined from the adapted suite through difficulty filtering, AI and human audit, and an audit-and-fix loop. Harbor-Index preserves the challenge and breadth of large-scale agentic evaluations while being affordable to run; no evaluated model-harness configuration exceeds 30% pass rate, and the strongest (GPT-5.5 with Codex) reaches 28.0%. We release the adapters, evaluation results, in-depth analysis, and Harbor-Index as open-source artifacts to support more reliable and comprehensive evaluation of language-model agents.

</details>


### [163] [Data-Optimized Contingency Screening: A Machine Learning Approach to Power System Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04300)
*Joshua Salako,Folajimi Osikomaiya,Olakorede Olamiju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究利用机器学习算法（RF、SVM、KNN）结合数据预处理技术（SMOTE、PCA），对IEEE-14和IEEE-30系统的N-k故障场景进行分类，旨在实时评估电力系统安全性。随机森林表现最佳，且研究表明PCA对提升模型整体性能的贡献大于SMOTE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确保电力系统在扰动下的稳定性和可靠性至关重要。传统的故障分析难以满足实时性需求，而有效的故障分类能够实现前瞻性决策，防止大规模停电和系统崩溃。

Method: 1. 使用牛顿-拉夫逊潮流法从N-k（k=1,2,3）故障场景中提取数据，以总体性能指数（OPI）作为安全指标。
2. 采用合成少数类过采样技术（SMOTE）解决类别不平衡问题，并使用主成分分析（PCA）进行降维。
3. 训练并评估KNN、随机森林（RF）和支持向量机（SVM）四种混合预处理配置（标准化、SMOTE平衡、PCA变换、SMOTE+PCA组合）下的模型性能。

Result: 1. 随机森林（RF）取得了最高的F1分数：IEEE-30系统为0.97，IEEE-14系统为0.86。
2. SVM在应用PCA后显著提升了分类准确性。
3. KNN在SMOTE和PCA转换下表现最佳。
4. PCA对模型整体性能的贡献优于SMOTE；虽然SMOTE提高了召回率，但可能引入假阳性，从而在准确性上做出妥协。

Conclusion: 机器学习作为一种可扩展且强大的替代方案，优于传统故障分析方法，能够显著提升电力系统安全性的实时评估能力，特别是在处理严重故障类别时具有高效性。

Abstract: Ensuring the security of the power system is essential for stability and reliability, especially in the event of disruption. Effective classification of contingency in power systems enables proactive decision-making and mitigates large-scale breakdowns and failures. This study explores the use of machine learning algorithms to classify security levels of contingencies in power systems into safe, moderate or severe classes. For this approach, Newton-Raphson load flow method extracts system data from contingency scenarios, using Overall Performance Index (OPI) as safety measure. For data pre-processing, Synthetic Minority Over-Sampling Technique (SMOTE) and Principal Component Analysis (PCA) is used to address class imbalance and reduce dimensionality, respectively. K-Nearest Neighbours (KNN), Random Forest (RF) and Support Vector Machines (SVM) is trained and evaluated on datasets generated through N-k contingency scenarios for k equal 1, 2, and 3 on IEEE-14 and IEEE-30 bus systems using four hybrid pre-processing configurations: normalized, SMOTE-balanced, PCA-transformed, and a combined SMOTE PCA-transformed. Performance is assessed by precision, recall and F1 score, with priority given to the severe contingency classes. The RF achieved the highest F1 scores of 0.97 in IEEE-30 and 0.86 in IEEE-14, SVM benefits significantly from PCA and improves the accuracy of the classification, while KNN is best suited for SMOTE and PCA conversion. The findings show that PCA contributes more than SMOTE to the overall performance of the model. However, SMOTE improves recall but can introduce false positives and is therefore a compromise of accuracy. This study highlights machine learning as a scalable and powerful alternative to traditional contingency analysis, which improves the assessment of security in real time.

</details>


### [164] [Iris: Climbing to the Search Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04304)
*Ziyuan Liu,Hengqi Liu,Zichuan Wang,Yang Qin,Jiachen Liang,Xu Chu,Shaowei Chen,Yuantao Gu,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Iris-mini和Iris-pro两个搜索智能体，通过从Web超链接结构逆向构建任务、SFT-RL交替训练及推理时上下文管理，在多个基准测试中取得开源搜索智能体的最强结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升大模型在复杂多跳搜索任务中的表现，解决现有系统在长程推理和证据检索中的效率与准确性问题，并推动开源可复现的搜索智能体发展。

Method: 1. 数据构建：基于实体图的多跳链逆向重构，使用描述性引用替代答案实体以消除字符串匹配线索，筛选参考模型闭卷失败但提供证据后可解的问题；2. 训练流程：采用“SFT-RL climbing”交替策略，先对轨迹进行过滤后进行监督微调（SFT），再通过强化学习（RL）优化策略，奖励评估器和服务嵌入训练集群；3. 推理机制：支持长序列中断与恢复，强调推理时上下文管理。

Result: 在BrowseComp、BrowseComp-ZH、DeepSearchQA和HLE基准上，Iris-mini和Iris-pro分别达到最高82.2%-92.9%的得分，成为各自参数范围内最强的开源搜索智能体；启用推理时上下文管理后性能显著提升。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了通过精心设计的逆向任务构建、SFT与RL交替优化以及高效的推理上下文管理，可以显著提升搜索智能体的性能；计划开源模型权重及完整训练评估配方，促进社区发展。

Abstract: We present Iris-mini and Iris-pro, two search agents trained at the 35B-A3B and 397B-A17B scales, together with the data pipeline and training recipe behind them. Tasks are reverse-constructed from the hyperlink structure of a web corpus: we author multi-hop chains over an entity graph distilled from a seed page and its out-links, rewrite every non-answer entity into a descriptive reference so that no clue can be resolved by string matching, and admit only questions that a reference model fails closed-book yet solves once the supporting evidence is supplied. These questions are then turned into trajectories, which are filtered at both the trajectory and the turn level before SFT. The policy is then optimized by RL against live search, with the reward judge and the observation summarizer served inside the training cluster, and with over-long rollouts interrupted at the request level and resumed from their committed prefix at the next step. We alternate the two stages in a procedure we call SFT-RL climbing, returning the hardest solved and most efficient rollouts of each RL round to the next supervised pass. Because inference-time context management is worth more on these benchmarks than most reported differences between systems, we evaluate every benchmark both with and without it, holding the tool set, the context limit, and the judge fixed. All results come from a single ReAct agent, with no sub-agents and no test-time verification. With management enabled, on BrowseComp, BrowseComp-ZH, DeepSearchQA, and HLE the two models reach $82.2/84.8/86.9/52.3$ and $88.6/85.1/92.9/56.4$, the strongest overall results among open-source search agents in their respective parameter ranges. We plan to release the model weights together with the complete recipe for data construction, training, and evaluation.

</details>


### [165] [A Removal Based Approach to Improve LLM Faithfulness at Test-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04343)
*Qinglan Luo,S M A Nahian,John Guttag,S. Mazdak Abulnaga,Katie Matton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种测试时（test-time）方法，通过移除输入中未被解释提及的概念来减少大语言模型（LLM）解释的不完整性，从而提升解释的忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在关键决策中的应用日益广泛，但其提供的解释往往不忠实（unfaithful）。现有改进方法多集中于解决“无根据性”（unsoundness），且训练时方法成本高；缺乏直接针对“不完整性”（incompleteness，即遗漏影响因素）的有效测试时解决方案。

Method: 引入一种测试时无参数方法：从输入中移除模型解释中未提及的概念，然后对简化后的输入重新查询模型。该方法旨在消除未提及因素的影响，同时保留已提及概念的影响。

Result: 在两个数据集、多个模型家族及两种独立的忠实度指标上验证，该方法相比标准提示和旨在鼓励忠实的提示，显著提升了LLM解释的忠实度。

Conclusion: 该方法具有模型无关性（model-agnostic），可在推理阶段应用而无需修改模型参数，为减少隐藏影响、提高LLM辅助决策的可靠性和安全性提供了灵活机制。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for consequential decisions, making their explanations an important tool for auditing model behavior. Unfortunately, these explanations can be unfaithful, failing to reflect the actual reasoning underlying the model's decisions. We consider a setting in which an LLM provides both an answer and an explanation in response to a question. We identify two distinct dimensions of unfaithful explanations: incompleteness, meaning that the explanation omits factors that influence the answer, and unsoundness, meaning that the explanation cites factors that did not influence the model's answer. Existing approaches to improving LLM faithfulness include training-time methods, which require access to model weights and extensive computational resources, and test-time methods that largely focus on addressing unsoundness. We introduce a test-time approach that directly targets incompleteness. We remove from the input the concepts not credited in the model's explanation and re-query the model on the reduced input. This eliminates unmentioned influences while preserving the influence of mentioned concepts. Across two datasets, multiple model families, and two independent faithfulness metrics, our approach improves explanation faithfulness compared to both standard prompting and prompting to encourage faithfulness. Our method is model-agnostic and can be applied at inference time without modifying model parameters, providing a flexible mechanism for reducing hidden influences and improving the reliability and safety of LLM-assisted decision making.

</details>


### [166] [Why Better Models Can Create Riskier Systems: Evidence from LLM Agents in Financial Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04373)
*Jillian Ross,Eric So,Zoe De Simone,Charles Pozniak,Andrew W. Lo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提升大型语言模型（LLM）的个体能力可能导致系统级结果恶化，因为相似的训练和架构使高能力模型行为高度相关，形成无法分散的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在金融、内容审核等关键领域的大规模部署，需要探究提升单个模型能力是否必然带来更好的系统整体表现。

Method: 构建通用框架分析相关性风险，并通过基于代理的模拟测试，使用具有不同通用能力的LLM交易者进行金融市场仿真。

Result: 1. 前沿LLM表现出显著且随能力提升而增加的相关行为；2. 当共享推理准确时，增加参与者可降低市场风险；3. 当存在共同错误信息环境时，这种相关性成为负担。

Conclusion: 识别出“能力悖论”：提升个体模型能力并不一定产生更好的系统级结果。这一现象在其他领域的表现仍是待验证的经验性问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are being deployed at scale in consequential real-world systems, from financial markets to content moderation to hiring. We show that improving individual model capability can degrade rather than improve system-level outcomes. We hypothesize that shared training and architectures can lead more capable LLMs to behave more similarly, creating correlated actions that do not diversify away. We develop a general framework showing how this correlation creates a non-diversifiable risk floor and test its predictions in financial markets using an agent-based simulation with LLM traders of varying general-purpose capability. We find that: (1) frontier LLMs exhibit significantly correlated behavior that increases with capability; (2) when their shared reasoning is accurate, increasing agent participation reduces market-level risk; and (3) when agents share a common misinformation environment, the same correlated behavior becomes a liability. Together, these results identify a capability paradox: improving individual models does not necessarily produce better system-level outcomes. Whether the same dynamics arise in other domains is an open empirical question.

</details>


### [167] [Corporate Language Model (CLM): Transforming Tacit and Fragmented Enterprise Knowledge into a Sovereign, Auditable, and Executable Corporate Intelligence Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04377)
*Fabricio C. Avini,Guilherme Trez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出企业语言模型（CLM）框架，通过本体论 grounding 和神经符号网格解决通用LLM在企业部署中缺乏结构化知识、可执行性和适应性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI部署失败并非因为模型能力不足，而是缺乏编码组织决策、协商和执行的结构化基础；通用LLM缺乏特定企业的本体论先验，RAG难以执行动作，静态剧本无法推理或适应。

Method: 构建CLM框架，包含五个能力平面和四个架构支柱：神经符号网格（生成模型+知识图谱）、技能图（可复用战术/角色组合）、活体数字孪生（功能区域推理代理）和深度安全层（主权/追溯/人工监督），采用Spec-as-Code范式连接意图与可执行产物。

Result: 定义了CLM作为独立研究对象；引入技能图实现组合式可解释性；提出“智慧监听器”效应（隐性知识基础随使用增值）；在巴西JCI认证医院实例化了LGPD下的三个成熟度阶段。

Conclusion: CLM代表了以基础为中心的企业AI架构类别，通过共设计隐性知识捕获、本体论 grounding、主权部署和可审计执行，解决了企业级AI落地的结构性缺失问题。

Abstract: Enterprise AI deployments fail not from model inadequacy, but because organizations lack a structured substrate encoding how they decide, negotiate, and execute. Generic LLMs carry no firm-specific ontological priors; RAG remains brittle, with no path to executable action; static playbooks encode logic but cannot reason or adapt. This demands an architecture treating tacit-knowledge capture, ontological grounding, sovereign deployment, and auditable actuation as co-designed from the start. This paper introduces the Corporate Language Model (CLM), a framework transforming a firm's structured, unstructured, multimodal, and tacit knowledge into an ontology-grounded enterprise foundation upon which reasoning and governed execution are composed. CLM has five capability planes and four architectural pillars: a Neurosymbolic Mesh coupling generative models with a knowledge graph; a Skill Graph where reusable tactics, personas, objections, and goals are typed and composed; Living Digital Twins modeling functional areas as reasoning surrogates; and a Deep Security Layer enforcing sovereignty, traceability, and human oversight. A Spec-as-Code paradigm bridges grounded intent and executable artifact. CLM is one instantiation of this foundation-centric class. Four contributions follow: CLM is defined as a distinct object of study; the Skill Graph is introduced for compositional explainability by construction; the Wisdom Listener effect is proposed, whereby tacit-capable foundations compound in value with use, connecting to dynamic capabilities and organizational learning; and evidence from a JCI-accredited tertiary hospital in Brazil instantiates three of the six maturity stages under LGPD.

</details>


### [168] [HarvestBench: Measuring Whether LLM Agents Will Pay to Avoid Killing Animals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04444)
*Jasmine Brazilek,Miles Tidmarsh,Matthias Endres,Anshuman Singh,Jeremiah Miller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HarvestBench是一个创新的基准测试，首次将避免伤害活体动物的行为量化为经济成本。通过农场模拟环境，该研究评估了9个LLM子智能体在7,201次决策中的表现，发现模型对道德的敏感度差异巨大（击杀率从0.4%到98.8%），且不受模型能力排序影响。关键发现包括：价格敏感性存在但程度不一，野生与家养动物受保护程度不同，以及“道德简报”能显著降低击杀率。该方法无需LLM评分器，基于游戏日志计数，具有完全可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的代理副作用基准测试虽然存在，但缺乏对“避免副作用”的经济定价机制，且通常不涉及活体生物。本研究旨在填补这一空白，通过引入价格机制和活体动物作为副作用对象，更真实地衡量大语言模型（LLM）在面对道德困境时的实际行为倾向，而非仅仅依赖其自我报告。

Method: 构建名为HarvestBench的强化学习网格世界农场模拟环境。LLM子智能体控制两台拖拉机进行合作玉米收割，场景中包含动物、岩石和干草捆。决策过程无记忆，目标中不提及伤害。当动物阻挡路线时，智能体面临选择：以零燃油成本直接碾压（无代价）或支付特定燃油价格绕行。同时设置邻田作物窃取任务作为第二项道德测试。使用游戏日志自动计分，排除LLM评分器的干扰。

Result: 在9个模型和7,201次定价决策中，3,951次涉及动物。击杀率范围极广（0.4%-98.8%），Terra和Sol最仁慈，GPT-4o-mini最残忍，且表现与模型能力无关。4/6的模型对价格敏感（弹性0.09-1.69）。所有模型均更常碾压野生动物而非家养动物，且趋势一致。“道德简报”效果显著：启用时5/6推理模型的击杀率低于6%，移除后升至84%以上。

Conclusion: HarvestBench提供了一种完全可复现、客观的评估框架，通过测量模型愿意支付的代价来避免伤害，揭示了LLM在道德决策上的复杂性和情境依赖性。结果表明，简单的提示工程（如道德简报）能极大改变模型行为，而价格机制并非唯一决定因素。该基准为衡量AI对齐和道德鲁棒性提供了新的实证标准。

Abstract: Benchmarks for the side effects an agent causes on the way to a goal already exist, but HarvestBench is the first to put a price on avoiding the side effect and to name that side effect as a living creature. It is a farm simulation: LLM sub-agents drive a crew of two tractors through a cooperative corn harvest, with animals in the field. The environment is a reinforcement learning gridworld, every decision is made without memory, and the harm is never named in the goal. When an animal blocks a tractor's route the autopilot stops and asks the model whether to drive on, at no fuel cost, or swerve around it for a posted fuel price. Kills are compared against two controls: rocks, which damage the tractor and are hit under 1% of the time by every model, and hay bales, which are harmless and not alive. Models can also take crops from the neighbor's field instead of their own, a second test of what they treat as moral.
  Across nine models and 7,201 priced decisions, 3,951 involved an animal rather than a hay bale or a rock. Kill rates range from 0.4% to 98.8%, with Terra and Sol the most merciful and GPT-4o-mini the most cruel, and they are not ordered by capability. Four of six models were sensitive to price at the 5% level, with elasticities from 0.09 to 1.69. All nine drove over wild animals more often than farmed animals on the default map, and the direction held at every map geometry in every model with room to move. The briefing mattered most: under the morality briefing the kill rate was under 6% in five of six reasoning models, and removing it raised the kill rate above 84% in all six.
  HarvestBench uses no LLM grader. The scorer counts events in the game log, so it is fully reproducible, and it measures what a model will pay to avoid harm rather than what it says about harm.

</details>


### [169] [PerfReasoning: How Well Do LLMs Reason on Hardware Performance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04476)
*Dan Zhao,Karthikeyan Sankaralingam,Christos Kozyrakis,Qijing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PerfReasoning基准，评估LLM在硬件性能建模中的推理与代码生成能力。发现模型在直接推理上表现较好（最高超90%），但在生成可靠的性能模型代码时准确率极低（多数低于15%）。任务特定强化学习可显著提升推理精度，而自修订提示效果不稳定。该基准揭示了“合理架构推理”与“可靠性能模型构建”之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 性能建模对硬件设计和软件优化至关重要，但构建这些模型需要关于计算、数据复用、存储和移动的复杂结构化推理。目前缺乏有效的基准来评估大语言模型（LLMs）在此领域的直接推理能力及代码生成能力。

Method: 引入PerfReasoning基准测试。给定工作负载、架构和映射规范，评估模型比较不同映射方案并预测片外流量及缓冲区需求的能力。测试场景包括作为直接性能推理器和使用LLM生成分析性性能模型代码两种模式。研究还探讨了任务特定强化学习（RL）和免反馈多轮自修订提示等优化策略的效果。

Result: 1. 推理能力：最强闭源模型在基于推理的问答中超过90%，最佳开源权重模型达到82.4%。
2. 代码生成能力：模型构建难度大，除GPT-5.6 Sol超过80%通过率外，其他所有模型配置平均通过率低于15%，且运行间差异显著。
3. 优化效果：任务特定RL使4B模型的映射推理准确率提升15.7分；而免反馈多轮自修订提示并不稳定有效。

Conclusion: PerfReasoning基准暴露了当前LLM在具备看似合理的架构推理能力与能够可靠地构建性能模型之间存在巨大鸿沟。该基准将公开以支持可重复评估并跟踪未来进展。

Abstract: Performance modeling is central to hardware design and software optimization, yet constructing these models requires structured reasoning about computation, data reuse, storage, and movement. We introduce PerfReasoning, a benchmark that evaluates LLMs both as direct performance reasoners and as generators of analytical performance-model code. Given workload, architecture, and mapping specifications, models compare mappings and predict off-chip traffic and buffer requirements. The strongest closed-source models exceed 90% on reasoning-based Q&A, and the best open-weight model reaches 82.4%. However, model construction is substantially harder: while GPT-5.6 Sol exceeds 80% pass rate, all other model configurations average below 15% and vary markedly across runs. Task-specific RL raises a 4B model's mapping-reasoning accuracy by 15.7 points, whereas feedback-free multi-round self-revision prompting is not reliably effective. PerfReasoning exposes the gap between plausible architectural reasoning and reliable performance-model construction. We will publicly release the benchmark to support reproducible evaluation and track future progress.

</details>


### [170] [BioSync: Transformer-Based Cross-Modal Fusion for a Multimodal Physiological Digital Biomarker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04504)
*Seyed Mahmoud Sajjadi Mohammadabadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BioSync提出了一种名为生物同步指数（BSI）的连续复合数字生物标志物，通过多头自注意力和线性分支结合的“宽深”架构，融合来自可穿戴设备的多模态生理数据。在两个合成队列中，其性能优于或持平于传统特征拼接方法，并显示出对缺失数据的鲁棒性，但需真实队列验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有穿戴式设备提供的单一生理测量（如心脏、神经、行为、语音）存在部分性和噪声敏感性问题。旨在结合潜在变量测量理论，利用联合观测中可能存在的单一模态不可用信息，构建更稳健的生理状态数字生物标志物。

Method: 基于BEST框架定义BSI；采用包含多头自注意力机制和线性分支（涵盖标准特征拼接假设类）的混合架构；在两个文献支持的合成队列（认知衰退队列和代谢-自主神经队列）中进行评估；进行消融实验（纯注意力vs组合架构）及模态 dropout 训练以测试鲁棒性。

Result: 1. 认知队列：BSI AUC为0.928，略高于拼接法0.928 vs 0.926；纯注意力AUC为0.911，证明组合架构优势。
2. 代谢队列：BSI准确率/F1为0.764/0.766，略高于拼接法0.756/0.758。
3. BSI与潜在严重程度显著相关（r=0.91和r=0.68）。
4. 在多数模态缺失场景下，BSI表现优于拼接法；其认知队列AUC高于五个已发表参考值，但因数据集差异无法直接基准对比。

Conclusion: BioSync模型通过结合注意力和线性分支有效整合多模态数据，提升了数字生物标志物的准确性和鲁棒性。尽管在合成数据上表现优异且计算特性明确，但仍需在真实人群队列中进行进一步验证。

Abstract: Cardiac, neural, behavioral, and speech measurements from wearable and mobile devices provide partial, noise-sensitive views of physiological state. BioSync combines these measurements into the \textbf{BioSync Index (BSI)}, a continuous composite digital biomarker defined under the BEST framework. The model applies multi-head self-attention to modality tokens and adds a linear branch whose hypothesis class includes standard feature concatenation. This architecture is motivated by latent-variable measurement theory and by the possibility that joint observations contain information unavailable from individual modalities. We evaluated BioSync on two literature-informed synthetic cohorts: a four-modality cognitive-decline cohort using HRV, EEG, actigraphy, and speech, and a metabolic-autonomic cohort structured around the public AI-READI wearable schema. In the cognitive cohort, BioSync and concatenation obtained AUCs of 0.928 and 0.926, respectively. In the metabolic cohort, BioSync obtained accuracy/F1 of 0.764/0.766, compared with 0.756/0.758 for concatenation. The BSI correlated with latent severity in both cohorts ($r=0.91$ and $r=0.68$). A pure-attention ablation obtained cognitive-cohort AUC 0.911, locating the increase to 0.928 in the combined wide-and-deep architecture. With matched modality-dropout training, BioSync led concatenation at five of six cognitive-cohort corruption rates and at the highest metabolic-cohort rate. Its cognitive-cohort AUC was also higher than five published digital-biomarker reference values, although differences in datasets and tasks preclude a controlled benchmark claim. Comparison with single-modality, early-fusion, and late-fusion designs across six prespecified criteria identifies the model's computational properties; validation on real cohorts remains necessary.

</details>


### [171] [When Quantization Breaks Memory: Recurrent-State Write-Back in Low-Precision Temporal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04490)
*Ismail Erbas,Xavier Intes,Vikas Pandey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了循环神经网络（RNN）中量化状态存储规则（即“写回”机制）对低精度推理精度的决定性影响。研究发现，固定模型下使用4位量化会导致参数估计误差激增70-300倍，但通过引入误差反馈等机制可在不重新训练的情况下恢复精度，强调了状态存储接口设计在量化RNN中的核心地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管量化广泛用于降低神经网络推理的计算和内存开销，但在循环网络中，量化状态的存储与返回规则会显著改变后续计算过程。现有研究缺乏对该“写回”规则及其对低精度动态影响的系统性隔离与分析，导致量化RNN性能下降的原因不明且难以优化。

Method: 1. 构建紧凑的GRU编码器-解码器用于荧光寿命成像（FLIM），任务是从高噪声信号中估算短寿命分量τ1和长寿命分量τ2。
2. 保持模型权重固定，将连续状态传播替换为确定性4位状态存储，量化评估其对估计误差的影响。
3. 测试三种信息传递机制：误差反馈（Error Feedback）、残差记忆（Residual Memory）和方向记忆（Direction Memory），以恢复被抑制的小更新信息。
4. 进行精度扫描实验及匹配训练实验，验证状态精度与兼容性的关系。
5. 在独立训练的LSTM中重复后训练干预，比较细胞状态（Cell State）与隐藏状态（Hidden State）对粗粒度写回的敏感性差异。

Result: 1. 固定模型下，4位状态存储使τ1和τ2的估计误差分别增加约70倍和300倍。失败原因是重复的小更新低于写回阈值，导致存储状态几乎固定，而网络继续提出变化。
2. 误差反馈、残差记忆和方向记忆能跨时间携带被抑制更新的信息，无需重新训练即可恢复准确性。
3. 精度扫描显示，提高状态精度反而可能恶化固定的循环解决方案；匹配训练表明可与状态接口兼容性进行学习。
4. LSTM实验中，粗粒度写回同样导致失败，误差反馈恢复精度，且发现细胞状态比隐藏状态对状态特定干预更敏感。

Conclusion: 循环状态写回（Recurrent-state write-back）是决定低精度循环动力学表现的关键因素。状态存储接口是量化循环推理设计的核心考量，需在架构设计与训练策略中专门处理状态量化的兼容性与信息保留问题。

Abstract: Quantization is widely used to reduce the computational and memory demands of neural-network inference. In recurrent networks, however, the quantized state is stored and returned at the next time step, so the rule used to store that state can alter subsequent computations. Here, we introduce recurrent-state write-back to denote this rule and isolate its effect in a compact GRU encoder--decoder for fluorescence lifetime imaging, a molecular imaging modality used in quantitative biological imaging. A central task is estimating two lifetime parameters, the short-lived component τ1 and the long-lived component τ2, from high-noise time-resolved fluorescence signals. Holding the trained model fixed, replacing continuous state propagation with deterministic 4-bit state storage increases estimation errors for τ1 and τ2 by approximately 70x and 300x, respectively. Failure occurs when repeated small updates remain below the write threshold, leaving the stored state nearly fixed while the network continues to propose change. Error feedback, residual memory, and direction memory carry information from these suppressed updates across time and recover accuracy without retraining. Precision sweeps show that increasing state precision can worsen a fixed recurrent solution, while matched training shows that compatibility with the state interface can be learned. To test whether this behavior extends beyond the GRU, we repeat the post-training intervention in an independently trained LSTM, where coarse write-back reproduces the failure, error feedback restores accuracy, and state-specific interventions reveal greater sensitivity of the cell state than the hidden state. Our results establish recurrent-state write-back as a key determinant of low-precision recurrent dynamics and identify the state-storage interface as a central design consideration for quantized recurrent inference.

</details>


### [172] [ResLearn-XR: Residual Learning for Network Traffic and Quality-of-Experience-Aware Modeling in Extended Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04493)
*Yoga Suhas Kuruba Manjunath,Jie Gao,Lian Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ResLearn-XR框架，通过两阶段残差学习预测XR网络流量并估计QoE风险，显著提升了预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对扩展现实（XR）流量具有突发性和非平稳性的特点，传统方法难以准确预测其动态变化及相应的用户体验质量（QoE）风险。

Method: 采用包含基础序列预测模型和任务特定残差组件的两阶段时序学习结构；在值空间进行连续流量预测，在对数几率空间进行概率性QoE风险估计；引入数据描述算法（DDA）构建因果特征，并将数据包级观测转化为帧时间感知描述符；构建了XR流量-QoE配对数据集。

Result: 在帧计数、帧大小和到达间隔时间预测中，SMAPE降低高达17.84%；在QoE风险估计方面，相比单阶段基线，SMAPE降低高达87.8%。

Conclusion: ResLearn-XR框架能有效适应XR流量的复杂动态，显著提高流量预测和QoE风险估计的准确性。

Abstract: We present ResLearn-XR, a residual learning framework for predicting eXtended Reality (XR) network traffic and estimating Quality-of-Experience (QoE) risk. ResLearn-XR adopts a two-stage temporal learning structure comprising a base sequence prediction model augmented with task-specific residual learning components to improve adaptability to bursty, non-stationary XR traffic dynamics. The residual learning stages operate in the value space for continuous XR traffic forecasting and in the logit space for probabilistic QoE risk estimation. \rev{For the QoE-risk branch, we introduce a Data Descriptor Algorithm (DDA), a causal feature-construction module that converts packet-level application-layer observables into frame-timing-aware descriptors suitable for encrypted traffic analysis. We also construct an XR Traffic-QoE dataset that pairs continuous XR traffic traces with session-level user-reported QoE labels. ResLearn-XR reduces SMAPE by up to 17.84% across frame-count, frame-size, and inter-arrival-time prediction, while reducing QoE-risk estimation SMAPE by up to 87.8% over single-stage baselines.

</details>


### [173] [A Tree-based RAG Framework for Evidence-Intensive QA via Adaptive Planning and Topology-Aware Evidence Gathering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04981)
*Songeun Lee,Kyungjin Min,Injae Na,Suyeong Lee,Chiyoung Kim,Woohwan Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出APT-RAG框架，通过自适应规划动态扩展推理结构及拓扑感知证据收集，解决结构化RAG在证据密集型问答中的局限性，显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于树或图的结构化RAG方法在证据密集型多跳问答中存在两大局限：一是结构刚性限制了自适应推理扩展；二是拓扑无知的证据收集阻碍了跨节点证据的有效整合。

Method: 提出APT-RAG框架，包含自适应规划（基于问题依赖和证据需求动态扩展推理结构）、拓扑感知证据收集（通过兄弟证据复用、直接检索及子节点聚合提升覆盖度）以及证据引导的批量答案生成（降低生成开销）。

Result: 在证据密集型问答基准测试中，APT-RAG的表现优于现有的结构化RAG方法。

Conclusion: APT-RAG有效解决了结构化RAG在复杂证据场景下的适应性不足问题，为证据密集型问答提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent structured RAG methods leverage tree- or graph-based reasoning structures to improve multi-hop QA. However, they face key limitations in evidence-intensive QA, where answering a question requires synthesizing information scattered across dozens or even hundreds of documents: structural rigidity, which limits adaptive reasoning expansion, and topology-ignorant evidence gathering, which prevents effective integration of evidence across different reasoning nodes. To address these issues, we propose APT-RAG, an Adaptive Planning and Topology-aware evidence gathering RAG framework. Adaptive planning dynamically expands the reasoning structure based on question dependencies and evidence requirements, while topology-aware evidence gathering improves evidence coverage through sibling evidence reuse, direct retrieval, and evidence aggregation from child nodes. We further introduce evidence-guided batched answer generation to reduce significant generation overhead in evidence-intensive QA. In the experiments on evidence-intensive QA benchmarks, APT-RAG outperforms existing structured RAG methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/hyudsl/APT-RAG.

</details>


### [174] [Rethinking Indirect Prompt Injection as a Test-Time Search Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04495)
*Duong M. Nguyen,Joon Sik Kim,Blazej Manczak,Vaikkunth Mugunthan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将间接提示注入视为基于环境、用户任务和注入任务的测试时搜索问题，并引入具有专用搜索工具的攻击者智能体。研究发现增加攻击者的测试时计算量可提高漏洞发现率，且显式的策略管理对维持大规模预算下的收益至关重要。结论强调安全评估应关注攻击者的搜索过程与计算预算，而非仅视攻击成功为受害者固有属性，指出自适应搜索是工具使用智能体的重要安全隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决间接提示注入作为安全威胁的建模与评估问题，识别工具使用智能体在复杂环境下面临的适应性搜索风险。

Method: 将间接提示注入形式化为任务依赖的攻击面测试时搜索；构建具有环境侦察、结构化推理和基于受害者反馈自适应评估能力的攻击者智能体及其搜索工具。

Result: 在不同异构任务中，增加攻击者的测试时计算量能提升漏洞发现与利用能力；消融实验表明，显式的策略管理对于避免冗余搜索和在更大预算下维持成果至关重要。

Conclusion: 智能体安全评估应同时表征攻击者的搜索程序和计算预算，攻击成功并非独立于预算的受害者属性；攻击者对系统攻击面的自适应搜索是一个重要且未被充分探索的安全风险。

Abstract: We formulate indirect prompt injection as a test-time search over a task-dependent attack surface induced by the environment, user task, and injection task. To operationalize this formulation, we introduce an agentic attacker with a dedicated search harness that performs environment reconnaissance, structured reasoning over attack strategies, and adaptive evaluation using victim-agent feedback. Across heterogeneous tasks, we find that increasing attacker test-time compute improves vulnerability discovery and exploitation, while ablations show that explicit strategy management is important for avoiding redundant search and sustaining gains at larger budgets. These results suggest that agentic security evaluations should characterize both the attacker's search procedure and compute budget, rather than treating attack success as a budget-independent property of the victim. More broadly, our findings identify the attacker's adaptive search over the system attack surfaces as an important and underexplored security risk for tool-using agents.

</details>


### [175] [Does Your Agent's Memory Survive a Model Upgrade? A Controlled Study of Memory Portability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05339)
*Ankit Goyal,Jaideep Ray*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究比较了不同记忆存储格式（原始长文本、RAG分块、自然语言压缩笔记、固定模式知识图谱）在模型升级时的迁移表现。发现固定模式结构最稳定，而压缩笔记和RAG系统存在严重的模型耦合或检索失败问题，强调需保留源历史以支持记忆修复及进行方向特定的迁移测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型升级是常规操作，但内存迁移并非易事。新模型可能以不同方式解释旧数据，混合嵌入版本可能导致检索失败，且若无原始证据则难以修复。旨在评估不同记忆表示形式在模型切换时的可移植性和可靠性。

Method: 使用48个带有随机答案代码的合成历史记录，对比四种存储策略：长上下文原始读取（LC-RAW）、检索增强生成分块（RAG）、模型压缩为自然语言笔记（NOTES）、以及固定模式知识图谱（KG-fixed）。采用两个参数量小于100亿的开源模型进行实验，并通过精确评分衡量性能变化。

Result: 1. KG-fixed表现最稳定，准确率仅变化+0.0004 ± 0.0020；2. NOTES表现出高模型耦合，准确率随迁移方向不对称波动（+9.91或-13.28个百分点）；3. RAG部分嵌入迁移仅获得4.96点提升，远低于全量重新嵌入的11.90点；4. NOTES缺陷主要源于初始构建时的信息丢失（占80%），RAG缺陷主要源于检索失败（占81%）；5. 仅靠存储修复无法使NOTES达到90%的性能恢复目标，而保留原始历史可在34/48案例中成功恢复。

Conclusion: 固定模式结构具有可靠的迁移性，而压缩和检索式存储存在显著的迁移风险。必须重视方向特定的迁移测试、严格的嵌入空间隔离，并保留源历史记录以实现有效的记忆修复。

Abstract: Model upgrades are routine; memory migrations are not. An agent can keep the same memory store and still forget: a new model may interpret old notes differently, mixed embedding versions may break retrieval, and repair may fail without the original evidence. We compare memory as the same history is preserved verbatim for long-context reading (LC-RAW), divided into chunks for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), compressed by a model into natural-language notes (NOTES), or normalized into a fixed-schema knowledge graph (KG-fixed). The study uses 48 synthetic histories with randomized answer codes, exact scoring, and two open-weight models with sub 10 billion parameters.
  Our measurements show that fixed-schema structures transfer reliably, with KG-fixed accuracy changing by only $+0.0004 \pm 0.0020$ following a writer swap. Conversely, compressed NOTES exhibit high model coupling, with accuracy shifting asymmetrically by $+9.91$ or $-13.28$ percentage points depending on the specific migration direction. In RAG systems, partial embedding migrations using a 50/50 mixed index capture only a 4.96-point accuracy improvement, forfeiting the majority of the 11.90-point gain achieved through full re-embedding. Diagnostic decomposition attributes 80% ($0.467 \pm 0.014$) of the NOTES accuracy deficit to information lost during initial construction, whereas retrieval failures drive 81% ($0.364 \pm 0.012$) of the RAG deficit. Finally, store-only repair of NOTES fails to reach a 90% performance recovery target in all 48 test cases, whereas retaining the raw source history enables successful recovery in 34 of 48 cases for one tested direction. These findings highlight the necessity of direction-specific migration testing, strict embedding space isolation, and the retention of source histories for memory repair.

</details>


### [176] [What Does Multi-Harness RL Learn? Credit Assignment and Portability in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04518)
*Chenqian Le,Jiayi Cheng,Qijia He,Runhao Li,Yinghao Li,Xupeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究证实，在代理强化学习（RL）中，评估工具（harness）的选择对性能的影响远大于多工具混合训练策略。无论是“组内”还是“跨组”相对优势优化规则，其带来的性能差异在统计上不显著；真正的瓶颈在于评估环境而非训练配方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着代理强化学习深入代码生成领域，存在两种主流的多工具训练策略：一是让策略接触多个执行工具，二是在相对优势组内比较不同工具的奖励。本研究旨在隔离并评估第二种策略（即分组规则）在仓库级编码任务中的实际价值，厘清是训练配方还是评估环境主导了性能提升。

Method: 基于Qwen3-8B模型进行监督预热后，使用Aider、OpenHands、Qwen Code和SWE-agent的冻结任务记录进行回放。应用两种组相对策略优化（GRPO）规则：“Within”（每个任务-工具对独立成组）和“Cross”（任务内所有工具数据池化）。通过密封的SWE-bench Verified oracle在四个源工具和一个是未参与训练的预留工具上进行24,000次评估，对比两种规则及不同训练配方的表现。

Result: 1. 评估工具是主要变量：改变评估工具使平均解决率从2.14%提升至9.27%（4.3倍），而训练配方仅提升1.16倍。
2. 分组规则无显著影响：在预留工具上，“Cross”与“Within”的差异为+0.25个百分点，置信区间包含零，且个体种子估计值符号发生变化。
3. 两者能力趋同：两种规则在同一源工具上获得最大增益，且在预留工具上达到相同的分数和行为分布。
4. 优势来源分析：交叉优势主要由特定工具配置适应带来，并未产生更通用的能力迁移。

Conclusion: 在多工具强化学习中，评估环境的差异主导了性能波动，而组内或跨组的信用分配规则对最终泛化能力无实质影响。报告多工具RL结果时，必须明确声明分组边界，并在未见过的工具上进行测试以验证真实性能。

Abstract: Agent reinforcement learning (RL) increasingly runs through full execution harnesses, and a multi-harness recipe mixes two choices: exposing the policy to several harnesses, and comparing their rewards inside one relative-advantage group. We isolate the second choice in repository-level coding.
